- Optimization routine run_improve(p, n, N, time_limit, rng, seed):
    p = np.clip(p,0); s=sum; if s<eps create central? else p/=s.
    v = np.log(p+1e-300) maybe not needed.
    best=p.copy(); best_score=fast_score(p)
    best_exact_score maybe evaluate_sequence occasionally? But final exact. Fast score reliable. We can exact evaluate best every e.g. 2s if n<=20000? For n=5000 direct 50M operations maybe 0.1s. But we can use evaluate_sequence long? It uses np.convolve O(n^2) in C; for 5000 okay. But if n large 20000, too slow. We'll final exact best once or few.
    eta=0.05; active_tol=0.02; warmup? No.
    stalled=0
    for iter while time < limit:
       q=fast_conv(p,N) (L)
       qmax=q.max(); score=2n*qmax (p sum1)
       if score < best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=score; stalled=0
       else: stalled++
       if stalled > some: perturb or change eta/tol; maybe accept worse with annealing?

def run_improve(p, n, time_limit, rng=None, seed=0):
    p = clip...
    if n<1 return p,inf
    S=p.sum(); if S<1e-12: p=central(n); S=...
    p=p/S
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=fast_score(p,n,N)
    eta=0.05
    min_eta=1e-4; max_eta=0.5
    active_tol=0.02
    stagn=0; last_best=best_score
    iter=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    # precompute maybe central? 
    while time < deadline:
       q = fast_q(p,n,N)
       qmax = float(np.max(q)); score=2*n*qmax
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=p.copy(); best_score=score; stagn=0
       else:
           stagn+=1
       if stagn > 200:
           # perturb: random small multiplicative noise or active_tol change
           p = perturb(p,rng)
           stagn=0
           # recompute q? continue
       # weights active threshold dynamic
       if active_tol<0.005: active_tol=0.02? maybe reduce to focus.
       w = make_weights(q,qmax,active_tol,n)
       d = grad_log(p,qmax,w,n,N)
       d = d - np.mean(d)
       # if d all zero break?
       norms = np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d))
       if norms < 1e-12: break
       # choose step: eta_step = eta / max(1.0, norms)?? if norms large.
       step = eta / max(1.0, norms*0.5) ???
       # But d normalized around 1; use eta directly.
       cand = p * np.exp(-eta*d)
       cand = np.clip(cand,1e-300,1e300)
       cs=cand.sum(); if cs<=0 or not finite: ...
       cand/=cs
       cseq=fast_score(cand,n,N)
       if cseq + 1e-12 < score:
           p=cand; eta*=1.05; if eta>max_eta: eta=max_eta
           stagn=0
       else:
           # try half, quarter
           accepted=False
           for mult in [0.5,0.25]:
              eta2=eta*mult
              cand2=...
              cseq2=...
              if cseq2 < score - 1e-12:
                 p=cand2; eta=max(min_eta, eta2*1.1); accepted=True; break
           if not accepted:
              eta*=0.5
              # if stagn high, try worse direction? add noise
              p = np.maximum(p*(1+small noise), floor)/sum? maybe accept if <best_score+0.01
       iter++
    # final exact evaluate? maybe in main.
    return best,best_score
```

```
def make_weights(q, qmax, tol, min_active):
    L=q.size; active = q >= qmax - tol*qmax - 1e-15
    cnt=active.sum()
    if cnt < min_active:
       # expand tol until cnt or K
       for t in [2*tol,5*tol,0.1,0.2,0.5]: ...
    if cnt==0: w[argmax]=1
    else: w[active]=1/cnt
    return w
```

Then p0=u0=-c <0, tail positive. Low q_k constant? For k, ∑p_i u_{k-i}. For central a*a=1, maybe correlation of a with u. If u anti-correlated with prefix, can reduce low q. This is exactly gradient direction: d_i proportional prefix_i; update u_i ∝ -d_i? Actually multiplicative log change ε u_i with u_i = -(A prefix_i -2) to first order. prefix_i decreasing with i, so u_i=-2 + A prefix_i flips sign for tail positive. So log-gradient is the optimal first-order direction. Good.

Maybe after a few gradient iterations, equivalent to a finite series. So implement gradient.

Detailed _central:
```
def _central(n):
    if n <=0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
    s=a.sum()
    return a/s
```
For large n, if a underflows? a_i~1/√n not underflow. Good.
Could use `math.comb` but recurrence.

Potential faster for many lengths: precompute? Not needed.

_fast_q:
```
def _fast_q(p, n, N, L):
   F=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
   q=np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:L]
   # due numerical, set tiny negative to 0
   q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
   return q
```
Using `F*F` not conjugate because squared real. If p real, FFT coefficients complex, square not |F|^2. Correct for polynomial square, not autocorr. Need F*F, not F * np.conj(F) (which would autocorr). Important. For convolution p*p, coefficient = ifft(FT(p)*FT(p)). Good.
For gradient correlation, Fw*conj(Fp).

_fast_score:
```
def _fast_score(p,n,N,L):
   q=_fast_q(p,n,N,L)
   maxq=float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0
   return 2.0*n*maxq
```
Assume p sum1. If p sum not1, need divide by S^2. We can include:
```
S=p.sum(); if S<=EPS return inf; maxq/S^2? But q computed on p unnormalized. score=2n*max(q)/S^2.
```
Use that for safety.

_gradient:
```
def _gradient_log(p, qmax, w, N, n):
   # p sum1, w sum1
   Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(Fw*np.conj(Fp), N)[:n]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   if qmax <= EPS:
       return np.zeros(n)
   d=2.0*c/qmax - 2.0
   # optionally center to reduce constant
   d -= float(np.mean(d))
   return d
```
Check if weights sum1. If p sum not1, formula M and S? We'll use p normalized inside optimize. For safety, if S not1, objective gradient of logM-2logS: d=∑w 2p_{k-i}/M - 2/S. In _gradient, pass S, M; if p not normalized. But we maintain p normalized. Use S=1.

_make_weights:
```
def _make_weights(q,qmax, active_tol, min_active):
   L=q.size
   if qmax <=0: return np.ones(L)/L
   min_active=min(L, max(1,min_active))
   for tol in [active_tol, 2*active_tol, 5*active_tol, 0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]:
      active = q >= qmax - tol*qmax - 1e-15
      cnt=int(active.sum())
      if cnt >= min_active:
         w=np.zeros(L); w[active]=1.0/cnt; return w
   # fallback top min_active by argpartition
   if min_active < L:
      idx=np.argpartition(q, L-min_active)[L-min_active:]
   else: idx=np.arange(L)
   w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0/len(idx)
   return w
```
If active_tol very small and central q first half equal to max, active count exactly n (assuming numerical), min_active maybe n//20? good. For initial, min_active = max(16, n//20) maybe <n, active count n. Good.
But if active count includes only one max due numerical (if central q not exact from FFT?), tol 0.02 will include near. Need ensure all first half within 0.02? Yes, they are mathematically equal; FFT roundoff maybe 1e-15. So active_tol=0.01 enough.

Pseudo _optimize_sequence:
Inputs p (normalized? no), n length maybe fixed. But if we want change length? The sequence length fixed. We'll call _optimize_sequence(p, time_limit, seed) where n=p.size.
```
def _optimize_sequence(p, time_limit, seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    if p.size==0: p=np.ones(1)
    n=p.size
    p=np.clip(p,0,None)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<EPS: p=_central(n); s=...
    else p=p/s
    L=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(L)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(best,n,N,L)
    # exact evaluate best? maybe not inside.
    eta=0.05; min_eta=1e-4; max_eta=2.0
    active_tol=0.02
    min_active=max(8, n//25)  # for n=5000 200
    stagn=0; no_improve=0
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_limit
    it=0
    while time < deadline:
       # if stagnant long, random restart/noise
       if stagn > 200:
           # add noise to log p
           sigma=0.2
           noise=rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n)
           p_new=np.exp(np.log(p+1e-300)+noise)
           p_new=np.clip(p_new,0,None); s=p_new.sum(); if s>EPS: p=p_new/s
           # maybe reset eta
           eta=max(0.01, min_eta*10)
           active_tol=0.05
           stagn=0
           # recompute? continue
       q=_fast_q(p,n,N,L); qmax=float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
       score=2.0*n*qmax  # p sum1
       if not np.isfinite(score): break
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 1000 and stagn<50:
           # smaller active_tol or bigger?
           pass
       w=_make_weights(q,qmax,active_tol,min_active)
       d=_gradient_log(p,qmax,w,N,n)
       dn=np.sqrt(float(np.mean(d*d)))
       if dn < 1e-10:
           # no gradient
           break? perturb
       # choose step based on dn
       # raw d centered. step eta scaled inversely with dn? If dn large, smaller.
       scale = max(1.0, dn) # if dn>1, reduce
       step=eta/scale
       accepted=False
       # try current, half, quarter
       for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125]:
           st=step*mult
           if st <=0: break
           exp_arg=-st*d
           # clip exp_arg to avoid overflow
           exp_arg=np.clip(exp_arg, -50.0, 50.0)
           cand=p*np.exp(exp_arg)
           cand=np.clip(cand, 1e-300, 1e300)
           cs=float(np.sum(cand))
           if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs<=EPS: continue
           cand=cand/cs
           cseq_score=_fast_score(cand,n,N,L)
           if np.isfinite(cseq_score) and cseq_score < score - 1e-12:
               p=cand; accepted=True
               # increase eta maybe
               eta*=1.1
               if eta>max_eta: eta=max_eta
               break
       if not accepted:
           eta*=0.7
           if eta < min_eta:
              # accept random perturbation maybe
              if no_improve > 200:
                 p=_perturb(p,rng,0.1)
                 eta=0.05
           else:
              stagn+=1
       else:
           if score < best_score: no_improve=0? already
       it+=1
       if it % 100 ==0:
           print(f'[iter {it}] n={n} score={best_score:.9f} cur={score:.9f} eta={eta:.5f} stagn={stagn}')
    return best,best_score
```
Problem: In loop, we compute score current p after possible accepted previous. If accepted based on cseq_score<score, score updates next iter. Good. If not accepted, p remains; score recomputed next iter same, no_improve increments indefinitely but p unchanged; we perturb after eta min. This may lead to same gradient and reject repeatedly. Need when eta too small and step no improvement, maybe take a step that improves a surrogate even if fast score worsens slightly? Or do gradient ascent in reverse? If current is local min of F but not global, need perturb. The stagn counter should increment when no accepted step (not no_improve). Set stagn when accepted=False. If accepted but score not best? We accept only if score < score (current), so every accept improves current; best improves maybe. If many accepts but score plateau (tiny), no_improve eventually. Use no_improve for best, stagn for step acceptance.

If no accepted step because all smaller eta cause score increase, the gradient direction might be due numerical/ties; need perturb. So if not accepted: eta*=0.5; no_step_count++; if no_step_count>20: perturb.

Also, active_tol and eta adapt:
- If score improving, maybe increase active_tol? Not.

```
def _sequence_from_global():
   seq = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
   if seq is None: return None
   try: arr=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
   except: return None
   if arr.size==0: return None
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,None)
   s=arr.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=EPS: return None
   return arr/s
```

def _make_weights(q,qmax,K):
   L=len(q)
   if K > L: K=L
   # Try tiny threshold
   th = qmax - max(1e-12*qmax, 1e-18)
   active = q >= th
   cnt=active.sum()

      if K < L:
         idx=np.argpartition(q, L-K)[L-K:]
      else: idx=np.arange(L)
      cnt=len(idx)
      w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1/cnt; return w
   elif cnt > K:
      idx=np.argpartition(q, L-K)[L-K:]  # top K
      w=zeros; w[idx]=1/K; return w
   else: w[active]=1/cnt
```

Perturb strategy:
```
def _perturb(p, rng, sigma):
    n=p.size
    noise=rng.normal(0, sigma, n)
    p2=np.exp(np.log(p+1e-300)+noise)
    p2=np.clip(p2,0,None)
    s=p2.sum()
    if s>EPS: p2/=s
    return p2
```

```
def _optimize_sequence(p, time_limit, seed=0):
    p = _make_valid(p)
    if p.size < 2: return p, INF? score
    n = p.size
    L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
    # initial best exact? fast.
    ...
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    eta = 0.1
    K = n
    K_patterns = [n, max(16, n*3//4), max(16, n//2), max(16, n*2//3), n*7//8?] but ensure <=L.
    pattern_idx=0; pattern_count=0
    no_step=0; no_best=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q = _fast_q(p,n,N,L)
        qmax=float(q.max())
        if not finite or qmax<=EPS: break
        score=2*n*qmax
        if score < best_score - 1e-10: best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_best=0
        else: no_best += 1
        # K pattern after some iterations
        if it % 500 == 0:
             pattern_idx=(pattern_idx+1)%len(K_patterns); K=K_patterns[pattern_idx]
        w = _make_weights_topK(q,K)  # no qmax needed? threshold small; top K fallback
        d = _gradient_log(p,qmax,w,N,n)
        if not finite variance: break
        d = d - np.mean(d)
        d_std = float(np.std(d))
        if d_std < 1e-9:
            # perturb
            p = _perturb(p,rng,0.1); no_step=0; continue
        d = d / d_std
        # clip
        d = np.clip(d, -10.0, 10.0)
        accepted=False
        # use 2 steps: eta, eta*0.5
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
            step=eta*mult
            if step <= 1e-7: break
            exparg = -step*d
            exparg = np.clip(exparg, -50.0, 50.0)
            cand = p * np.exp(exparg)
            s = float(np.sum(cand))
            if not finite or s<EPS: continue
            cand = cand/s
            cscore = _fast_score(cand,n,N,L)
            if finite and cscore < score - 1e-12:
                 p=cand; accepted=True
                 eta = min(max_eta, eta*1.08)
                 no_step=0
                 break
        if not accepted:
            no_step +=1
            eta = max(min_eta, eta*0.7)
            if no_step > 15 or (no_best > 1000 and no_step>5):
                 # perturb based on no_best
                 sigma = 0.05 if no_best < 3000 else 0.15
                 p = _perturb(p, rng, sigma)
                 # maybe reset best? best remains
                 no_step=0
                 eta=0.05
        it +=1
        if it % 250 == 0:
             print(f'opt n={n} it={it} best={best_score:.9f} score={score:.9f} eta={eta:.4f} K={K}', flush=True)
    best = _make_valid(best)
    return best, best_score
```

_make_weights: 
```
def _make_weights(q, K):
   L=q.size
   if K is None or K<=0 or K>=L:
      return np.ones(L)/L
   qmax=float(np.max(q))
   if qmax <= 0: return np.ones(L)/L
   tol=max(1e-13, 1e-8*qmax)
   active = q >= qmax - tol
   cnt=int(active.sum())
   if cnt <= 0:
      w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(q))]=1; return w
   if cnt < K:
      if K < L:
         idx=np.argpartition(q, L-K)[L-K:]
      else:
         idx=np.arange(L)
   elif cnt > K and cnt > int(1.25*K) + 10:
      idx=np.argpartition(q, L-K)[L-K:]
   else:
      w=np.zeros(L); w[active]=1.0/cnt; return w
   w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0/len(idx); return w
```

Let's write helper `_normalize_sequence`:
```
def _normalize_sequence(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1e300)
    s=p.sum()
    if not finite or s<=EPS:
       # fallback ones
       return np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64)/max(1,p.size)
    return p/s
```
But if p all zeros due invalid, returns uniform. In propose, if invalid, maybe central. Use `_make_valid` similar. Need distinguish invalid length? It's okay.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: budget_s=float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s=2400.0
    if budget_s <=3: budget_s=3.0
    deadline=t0+budget_s-3.0
    try: seed_int=int(seed)
    except: seed_int=42
    # build candidates
    cand_list=[]
    gseq=_global_sequence()
    main_len=4498
    if gseq is not None and gseq.size>0:
        cand_list.append(gseq)
        if 50 <= gseq.size <= 12000:
            main_len=gseq.size
    # variants
    if main_len < 2: main_len=64
    a=_central_base(main_len)
    cand_list.append(a.copy())
    ar=a[::-1]
    for r in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,3.0):
        cand_list.append(a + r*ar)
    steps=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,main_len)
    for t in (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
        cand_list.append(a*(1.0 + t*steps))
    steps2=steps*steps
    for t in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
        cand_list.append(a*(1.0 + t*steps2))
    for g in (-0.5,-0.2,0.2,0.8,1.2):
        cand_list.append(np.power(a, g))
    # maybe if global length matches but not main, no.
    best_arr=None; best_score=np.inf
    for arr in cand_list:
       p=_normalize_sequence(arr)
       n=p.size
       if n<1 or n>20000: continue
       L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
       sc=_fast_score(p,n,N,L)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
           best_score=sc; best_arr=p.copy()
    if best_arr is None:
       best_arr=_central(64); best_score=np.inf
    n=best_arr.size
    if n<100 and time? maybe choose 4498 variants? already.

    # phase 2 sym if time
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 120 and best_arr.size <= 15000:
        psy=_symmetrize(best_arr)
        if np.mean(np.abs(psy-best_arr)) > 1e-6:
            t2=min(300.0, 0.2*remaining)
            if t2>30:
               p2,s2=_optimize_sequence(psy,t2,seed_int+1)
               if s2<best_score: best_arr=p2; best_score=s2
    # phase 3 larger central variants if time
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 120:
        n3=int(min(12000, max(8192, int(best_arr.size*1.5))))
        a3=_central_base(n3)
        # choose best among a3 and a3+rev? score quickly
        cand3=[a3.copy(), a3+a3[::-1], a3*(1+np.linspace(0,1,n3))]
        # maybe also central normalized

        for arr in cand3:
           p=_normalize_sequence(arr)
           N3=_next_pow2(2*n3-1)
           sc=_fast_score(p,n3,N3,2*n3-1)
           if sc<s3: s3=sc; p3=p.copy()
        t3=min(400.0, 0.25*remaining)
        if t3>30:
           p3,s3=_optimize_sequence(p3,t3,seed_int+2)
           if s3<best_score: best_arr=p3; best_score=s3
    # phase 4 random restart from best if time
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 60:
        rng=np.random.default_rng(seed_int+99)
        # perturb log with sigma 0.2
        p4=_perturb(best_arr, rng, 0.15)
        t4=min(200.0, 0.3*remaining)
        if t4>20:
           p4,s4=_optimize_sequence(p4,t4,seed_int+3)
           if s4<best_score: best_arr=p4; best_score=s4
    # final validation
    best_arr=_normalize_sequence(best_arr)
    if best_arr.size > 20000: # if too large? can't reduce? Maybe truncate? But evaluator slow. We can keep if from height? If >20000 not optimized; perhaps reduce? Better to return smaller central? Need ensure final length not too slow. Limit final length to 20000 by choosing best among valid candidates. If best_arr from global >20000, maybe use central 4498 instead. So in candidate loop we skipped >20000, best_arr won't be >20000. Good.
    # exact check

    try:

    except Exception:

    print(f'final score approximate {best_score} exact {exact} length {len(seq_list)}')

Let's write `_perturb` carefully:
```
def _perturb(p, rng, sigma):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    lp=np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    noise=rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=p.shape)
    p2=np.exp(lp+noise)
    p2=np.clip(p2,0.0,1e300)
    s=float(np.sum(p2))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=EPS:
       return p.copy()
    return p2/s
```
If p contains zero, log floor. Good.

Symmetrize:
```
def _symmetrize(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel(); n=p.size
    if n<=1: return p.copy()
    q=0.5*(p+p[::-1])
    s=q.sum(); if s>EPS: return q/s
    return p.copy()
```

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    n=1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n
```

```
def _global_sequence():
    seq = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if seq is None: return None
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return None
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1e300)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not finite or s<=EPS: return None
    return arr/s
```

def _optimize_sequence(p, time_limit, seed=0):
    p = _normalize_sequence(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
       p=np.ones(1); n=1
    L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(best,n,N,L)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_score=np.inf
    if float(time_limit) <= 0.0 or n <=1:
       return best,best_score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+float(time_limit)
    eta=0.1; min_eta=1e-4; max_eta=1.0
    K_default=n; K=K_default
    K_alts=[]
    if n>32:
       K_alts=[max(16, 3*n//4), max(16, n//2), max(16, 2*n//3), max(16, 5*n//8)]
    alt_idx=0; alt_left=0
    no_step=0; no_best=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       q=_fast_q(p,n,N,L)
       qmax=float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
       score=2.0*n*qmax
       if not np.isfinite(score): break
       if score > best_score + 0.05:
           p=best.copy(); continue
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_best=0
       else:
           no_best += 1
       # K selection
       if alt_left > 0:
           alt_left -= 1
           if alt_left == 0:
               K = K_default
       else:
           K = K_default
           if K_alts and no_best > 1500:
               K = K_alts[alt_idx % len(K_alts)]
               alt_idx += 1
               alt_left = 300
       w=_make_weights(q,K)
       d=_gradient_log(p,qmax,w,N,n)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)): d=np.zeros(n)
       d = d - float(np.mean(d))
       std=float(np.std(d))
       if not np.isfinite(std) or std < 1e-9:
           p=_perturb(p,rng,0.1)
           no_step=0
           continue
       d = d/std
       d = np.clip(d, -4.0, 4.0)
       accepted=False
       for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
           step=eta*mult
           if step <= 1e-8: break
           exp_arg=-step*d
           exp_arg=np.clip(exp_arg, -15.0, 15.0)
           cand=p * np.exp(exp_arg)
           cand=np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1e300)
           s=float(np.sum(cand))
           if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= EPS: continue
           cand = cand/s
           cscore=_fast_score(cand,n,N,L)
           if np.isfinite(cscore) and cscore < score - 1e-12:
               p=cand; accepted=True; no_step=0
               eta=min(max_eta, eta*1.08)
               break
       if not accepted:
           no_step += 1
           eta=max(min_eta, eta*0.75)
           if no_step > 10 or (no_best > 2000 and no_step > 3):
               sigma=0.05 if no_best < 3000 else 0.15
               p=_perturb(p,rng,sigma)
               no_step=0
               eta=0.05
       it += 1
       if it % 250 == 0:
           print('opt n={} it={} best={:.10f} score={:.10f} eta={:.5f} K={}'.format(n,it,best_score,score,eta,K), flush=True)
    best=_normalize_sequence(best)
    return best,best_score
```

Implement `_make_weights_softmax(q, rel_tau)`:
```
def _make_weights_softmax(q, rel_tau=1e-6):
   qmax=float(np.max(q))
   L=q.size
   if not finite or qmax<=0: return np.ones(L)/L
   temp=max(1e-15, qmax*rel_tau)
   z=(q-qmax)/temp
   z=np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
   w=np.exp(z)
   s=float(np.sum(w))
   if s<=EPS or not finite:
      w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(q))]=1
   else: w/=s
   return w
```
This is cheap. No sorting. Good.

```
def _upsample(p, m):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    m=int(m)
    if m <=0: return p
    if p.size == m: q=p.copy()
    elif p.size ==1: q=np.full(m,p[0])
    else:
        x_old=np.arange(p.size,dtype=float)
        x_new=np.linspace(0.0, float(p.size-1), m)
        q=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    s=q.sum(); if s>EPS: q/=s
    return q
```

```python
"""
Local search / mirror descent for nonnegative sequences minimizing
2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The code starts from several analytic seeds (central-binomial truncations and
simple variations), then applies a smoothed projected mirror descent method in
log-space.  Convolution and gradient-like terms are computed with FFTs.  The
final sequence is normalized, clipped, and returned.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np

EPS = 1e-12


def _next_pow2(m):
    n = 1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def _normalize_sequence(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1e300)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= EPS:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s


def _global_sequence():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1e300)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= EPS:
        return None
    return arr / s


def _central_base(n):
    """Unnormalized coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2) truncated to length n."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return a


def _central(n):
    p = _central_base(n)
    return _normalize_sequence(p)


def _fast_q(p, n, N, L):
    """Linear convolution p*p by FFT, truncated to length L=2*n-1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:L]
    return np.maximum(q, 0.0)


def _fast_score(p, n, N, L):
    """Scale-invariant fast evaluation of the objective."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= EPS:
        return np.inf
    q = _fast_q(p, n, N, L)
    if q.size == 0:
        return 2.0 * float(n)
    mq = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(mq):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mq / (s * s)


def _make_weights_softmax(q, rel_tau=1e-6):
    """Smooth surrogate weights concentrated on near-maximal convolution lags."""
    L = int(q.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    temp = max(1e-15, qmax * rel_tau)
    z = (q - qmax) / temp
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= EPS:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def _gradient_log(p, qmax, w, N, n):
    """
    Gradient of log(max_q) - 2 log(sum p) w.r.t. log(p), using weighted lags.
    Requires p sum 1.
    """
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fw * np.conjugate(Fp), N)[:n]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if qmax <= EPS:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d = 2.0 * c / qmax - 2.0
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    return d


def _perturb(p, rng, sigma):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=p.size)
    p2 = np.exp(lp + noise)
    p2 = np.clip(p2, 0.0, 1e300)
    s = float(np.sum(p2))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= EPS:
        return p.copy()
    return p2 / s


def _symmetrize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size <= 1:
        return p.copy()
    q = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if math.isfinite(s) and s > EPS:
        return q / s
    return p.copy()


def _upsample(p, m):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return p.copy()
    if p.size == m:
        q = p.copy()
    elif p.size == 1:
        q = np.full(m, float(p[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x_old = np.arange(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(p.size - 1), m)
        q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return _normalize_sequence(q)


def _optimize_sequence(p, time_limit, seed=0):
    """
    Smoothed mirror descent on normalized nonnegative sequences.

    Returns (best_sequence, best_fast_score).
    """
    p = _normalize_sequence(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        p = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        n = 1

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score(best, n, N, L)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = np.inf

    if float(time_limit) <= 0.0 or n <= 1:
        return best, best_score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_limit)

    eta = 0.10
    min_eta = 1e-4
    max_eta = 1.0

    base_tau = 1e-6
    tau_alts = [1e-5, 1e-4, 1e-3, 5e-6]
    tau_alt_idx = 0
    tau_alt_left = 0
    tau = base_tau

    no_step = 0
    no_best = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q = _fast_q(p, n, N, L)
        qmax = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * qmax

        if not math.isfinite(score):
            break

        # If random perturbation made us poor, restore best and try again.
        if score > best_score + 0.05:
            p = best.copy()
            no_step += 1
            if no_step > 20:
                p = _perturb(p, rng, 0.10)
                no_step = 0
            continue

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = score
            no_best = 0
        else:
            no_best += 1

        if tau_alt_left > 0:
            tau_alt_left -= 1
            if tau_alt_left == 0:
                tau = base_tau
        else:
            tau = base_tau
            if no_best > 1500:
                tau = tau_alts[tau_alt_idx % len(tau_alts)]
                tau_alt_idx += 1
                tau_alt_left = 300

        w = _make_weights_softmax(q, tau)
        d = _gradient_log(p, qmax, w, N, n)

        d = d - float(np.mean(d))
        std = float(np.std(d))
        if not math.isfinite(std) or std < 1e-9:
            p = _perturb(p, rng, 0.10)
            no_step = 0
            continue

        d = d / std
        d = np.clip(d, -4.0, 4.0)

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            step = eta * mult
            if step <= 1e-8:
                break
            exp_arg = -step * d
            exp_arg = np.clip(exp_arg, -15.0, 15.0)
            cand = p * np.exp(exp_arg)
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1e300)
            s = float(np.sum(cand))
            if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= EPS:
                continue
            cand = cand / s
            cscore = _fast_score(cand, n, N, L)
            if math.isfinite(cscore) and cscore < score - 1e-12:
                p = cand
                accepted = True
                no_step = 0
                eta = min(max_eta, eta * 1.08)
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_step += 1
            eta = max(min_eta, eta * 0.75)
            if no_step > 10 or (no_best > 2000 and no_step > 3):
                sigma = 0.05 if no_best < 3000 else 0.15
                p = _perturb(p, rng, sigma)
                no_step = 0
                eta = 0.05

        it += 1
        if it % 250 == 0:
            print(
                f"opt n={n} it={it} best={best_score:.10f} "
                f"score={score:.10f} eta={eta:.5f} tau={tau:.2e}",
                flush=True,
            )

    best = _normalize_sequence(best)
    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing the given evaluation function.

    This function is invoked by the judge.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    if budget_s <= 3.0:
        budget_s = 3.0
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 4.0

    try:
        seed_int = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_int = 42

    candidates = []

    gseq = _global_sequence()
    main_len = 4498
    if gseq is not None and gseq.size > 0:
        candidates.append(gseq)
        if 50 <= gseq.size <= 12000:
            main_len = int(gseq.size)

    if main_len < 2:
        main_len = 64
    main_len = int(min(12000, max(64, main_len)))

    a = _central_base(main_len)
    ar = a[::-1]
    steps = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, main_len, dtype=np.float64)
    steps2 = steps * steps

    candidates.append(a.copy())

    # Seed family: linear mixture with reverse, tail boosts, power shapes.
    for r in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 3.0):
        candidates.append(a + r * ar)

    for t in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        candidates.append(a * (1.0 + t * steps))

    for t in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        candidates.append(a * (1.0 + t * steps2))

    for g in (-0.5, -0.2, 0.2, 0.8, 1.2):
        candidates.append(np.power(a, g))

    best_arr = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # Choose the best starting point by fast FFT score.
    for arr in candidates:
        p = _normalize_sequence(arr)
        n = int(p.size)
        if n < 1 or n > 20000:
            continue
        L = 2 * n - 1
        N = _next_pow2(L)
        sc = _fast_score(p, n, N, L)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = p.copy()

    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = _central(main_len)
        best_score = np.inf

    # Phase 1: main local search.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        base_t1 = 0.60 * rem
        if best_score < 1.51:
            base_t1 = min(base_t1, 300.0)
        t1 = max(0.0, min(rem - 5.0, base_t1))
        if t1 > 30.0:
            p1, s1 = _optimize_sequence(best_arr, t1, seed_int)
            if s1 < best_score:
                best_arr = p1
                best_score = s1

    # Phase 2: symmetric variant of the current best.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0 and best_arr.size <= 15000:
        psy = _symmetrize(best_arr)
        if float(np.mean(np.abs(psy - best_arr))) > 1e-7:
            t2 = min(350.0, 0.25 * rem)
            if t2 > 30.0:
                p2, s2 = _optimize_sequence(psy, t2, seed_int + 1)
                if s2 < best_score:
                    best_arr = p2
                    best_score = s2

    # Phase 3: try a longer constructed/upsampled sequence.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0:
        n3 = int(min(12000, max(8192, int(best_arr.size * 1.5))))
        if n3 > best_arr.size:
            base3 = _central_base(n3)
            steps3 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n3, dtype=np.float64)
            ups = _upsample(best_arr, n3)

            cands3 = [
                ups.copy(),
                _central(n3),
                base3 * (1.0 + np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n3, dtype=np.float64)),
                _normalize_sequence(ups + ups[::-1]),
                _normalize_sequence(ups * (1.0 + 0.5 * steps3)),
            ]

            p3 = None
            s3 = np.inf
            N3 = _next_pow2(2 * n3 - 1)
            L3 = 2 * n3 - 1
            for arr in cands3:
                pp = _normalize_sequence(arr)
                sc = _fast_score(pp, n3, N3, L3)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < s3:
                    s3 = sc
                    p3 = pp.copy()

            if p3 is not None:
                t3 = min(550.0, 0.35 * rem)
                if t3 > 30.0:
                    p3, s3 = _optimize_sequence(p3, t3, seed_int + 2)
                    if s3 < best_score:
                        best_arr = p3
                        best_score = s3

    # Phase 4: final random restart from the best known sequence.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        rng4 = np.random.default_rng(seed_int + 99)
        p4 = _perturb(best_arr, rng4, 0.12)
        t4 = min(250.0, rem - 5.0)
        if t4 > 20.0:
            p4, s4 = _optimize_sequence(p4, t4, seed_int + 3)
            if s4 < best_score:
                best_arr = p4
                best_score = s4

    best_arr = _normalize_sequence(best_arr)
    best_arr = np.clip(best_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    seq_list = [float(x) for x in best_arr]

    exact = float("nan")
    if "evaluate_sequence" in globals():
        try:
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence(seq_list))
        except Exception:
            exact = float("nan")

    if exact is not float("nan"):
        pass

    # Very conservative fallback in case something unexpected happened.
    if not seq_list:
        seq_list = [float(x) for x in _central(64)]

    print(f"final length={len(seq_list)} fast={best_score:.10f} exact={exact}", flush=True)
    return seq_list
```
